                                                                                   Frequently asked questions
                                                        Trials and purchases

                                                        How does the 30-day trial work?

                                                        A 30-day trial period is available for our DeepL Pro Starter, DeepL Pro Advanced and DeepL Write
                                                        Pro plans. You won’t have to pay anything until the end of the 30 days, and you can cancel at any
                                                        time. Trials aren’t available to users who have previously received a DeepL trial.

                                                           Trials and purchases

                                                           How does the 30-day trial work?

                                                           A 30-day trial period is available for our DeepL Pro Starter, DeepL Pro Advanced and DeepL Write
                                                           Pro plans. You won’t have to pay anything until the end of the 30 days, and you can cancel at any
                                                           time. Trials aren’t available to users who have previously received a DeepL trial.


                                                           If I have a DeepL Pro subscription, can I get a 30-day trial for DeepL Write Pro?

                                                        Trials and purchases

                                                        How does the 30-day trial work?

                                                        A 30-day trial period is available for our DeepL Pro Starter, DeepL Pro Advanced and DeepL Write
                                                        Pro plans. You won’t have to pay anything until the end of the 30 days, and you can cancel at any
                                                        time. Trials aren’t available to users who have previously received a DeepL trial.

                                                              Trials and purchases

                                                              How does the 30-day trial work?

                                                              A 30-day trial period is available for our DeepL Pro Starter, DeepL Pro Advanced and DeepL Write
                                                              Pro plans. You won’t have to pay anything until the end of the 30 days, and you can cancel at any
                                                              time. Trials aren’t available to users who have previously received a DeepL trial.


                                                              If I have a DeepL Pro subscription, can I get a 30-day trial for DeepL Write Pro?
